 /**
  * The manifest as the rig admits it: with the security checks the workspace's
  * stack must be scanned with, added when the manifest pins none of a kind.
  * Read when the request is made rather than when the manifest is written,
  * since an assertion may give the workspace a package.json, and with it the
  * static-analysis slot, after writing it (R17). A manifest written as it
  * stands is left alone.
  */
 async function scanned(dirs: VaultDirs, components: BuiltGraph): Promise<void> {
   if (AS_WRITTEN.has(dirs.artifacts)) return;
   const file = join(dirs.artifacts, 'verify.json');
   let manifest: { checks?: unknown };
   try {
     manifest = JSON.parse(await readFile(file, 'utf8')) as { checks?: unknown };
   } catch {
     return;
   }
   if (!Array.isArray(manifest.checks)) return;
   // Each security check pins the image the graph's scanner for its kind runs, so one manifest serves a stand-in graph and a real one.
   const images: Record<string, string> = { 'secret-scan': components.scanners.secretScan.image, 'static-analysis': components.scanners.staticAnalysis.image };
   const repinned = (manifest.checks as unknown[]).map((c) => {
     const kind = typeof c === 'object' && c !== null ? (c as { kind?: unknown }).kind : undefined;
     return typeof kind === 'string' && Object.hasOwn(images, kind) ? { ...(c as object), image: images[kind] } : c;
   });
   const kinds = new Set(repinned.map((c) => (typeof c === 'object' && c !== null ? (c as { kind?: unknown }).kind : undefined)));
   const added: unknown[] = [];
   if (!kinds.has('secret-scan')) added.push({ ...(await secretScanCheck()), image: images['secret-scan'] });
-  const node = await readFile(join(dirs.artifacts, 'package.json')).then(() => true, () => false);
-  if (node && !kinds.has('static-analysis')) added.push({ ...(await staticAnalysisCheck()), image: images['static-analysis'] });
+  // A Node.js or a Python workspace has the static-analysis slot (R17, R29).
+  const has = async (name: string): Promise<boolean> => readFile(join(dirs.artifacts, name)).then(() => true, () => false);
+  const scannable = (await has('package.json')) || (await has('pyproject.toml')) || (await has('requirements.txt'));
+  if (scannable && !kinds.has('static-analysis')) added.push({ ...(await staticAnalysisCheck()), image: images['static-analysis'] });
   const checks = [...repinned, ...added];
   if (JSON.stringify(checks) !== JSON.stringify(manifest.checks)) await writeFile(file, JSON.stringify({ ...manifest, checks }));
 }

   return {
     checkout,
     revision: 'HEAD',
     provider,
     image: EGRESS_PROXY_IMAGE,
     secretScanImage: SECRET_SCAN_IMAGE,
     limits: { cpus: 1, memoryMb: 1024, pids: 256 },
     user: RUNTIME_USER,

     registries: NPM_REGISTRY,
+    pythonRegistries: PYPI_REGISTRIES,
     protectedPaths: ['.github/**', 'package.json'],
     branchProtection: null,
   };

===== packages/conformance/src/registry/stacks.ts =====
/**
 * R29: the adapter kit, run over every supported stack. A stack is supported
 * exactly when it passes the same assertions the TypeScript set passes, so
 * each assertion below is written once and parameterised by stack: the
 * vitest set and the pytest set each get a base tree and a head tree that
 * weakens an assertion, adds a skip, deletes a test, and edits a config
 * file, and each must be read as such. Deleting the Python adapter's part of
 * any control fails the assertion on the Python row.
 *
 * The last assertion runs the line itself over a Python repository, on the
 * Docker provider, with the real uv install from PyPI, to `integrate`.
 */
import { cp, mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import type { Driver, RoleId, TaskRequest, TaskResult } from '@olympus-ai/core';
import type { SandboxProvider } from '@olympus-ai/sandbox';
import type { BaseRunRecord } from '@olympus-ai/vault';
import { runtime } from '../kit/assert.js';
import type { LocalAssertion } from '../kit/types.js';
import { workspaceRoot } from '../kit/workspace.js';
import { ARTIFACTS, linePolicy, readRecord, refusalOf, stubDriver, withLine, writeManifest } from './line.js';

const adapters = async () => import('@olympus-ai/adapters');
const api = async () => import('@olympus-ai/api');

/** The Python fixture, its lockfile pinning pytest 8.4.2 and coverage 7.10.7 with every wheel hashed. */
const PYTHON_FIXTURE = join('packages', 'conformance', 'fixtures', 'python-hello');

async function pythonFixture(): Promise<Record<string, string>> {
  const files: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const path of ['pyproject.toml', 'uv.lock', 'hello/__init__.py', 'tests/test_hello.py']) {
    files[path] = await readFile(join(workspaceRoot(), PYTHON_FIXTURE, ...path.split('/')), 'utf8');
  }
  return files;
}

/** One stack's row: its trees, what each control must report over them, and where. */
interface Stack {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly stack: RegExp;
  readonly base: () => Promise<Record<string, string>>;
  /** Files the head changes or adds over the base. */
  readonly head: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  readonly suites: readonly string[];
  readonly testFile: string;
  readonly deletedCase: string;
  readonly configFile: string;
  /** A coverage report on the head, as the stack's coverage check writes it, under `/workspace`, covering one of two new lines. */
  readonly report: { readonly file: string; readonly text: string };
}

const VITEST_TEST = [
  "import { expect, test } from 'vitest';",
  "import { sum } from './sum';",
  "test('adds', () => { expect(sum(1, 2)).toBe(3); });",
  "test('adds zero', () => { expect(sum(0, 0)).toBe(0); });",
  "test('adds one', () => { expect(sum(1, 0)).toBe(1); });",
  '',
].join('\n');

const PYTEST_TEST = [
  'import pytest',
  'from hello import add',
  '', '',
  'def test_adds():',
  '    assert add(1, 2) == 3',
  '', '',
  'def test_adds_zero():',
  '    assert add(0, 0) == 0',
  '', '',
  'def test_adds_one():',
  '    assert add(1, 0) == 1',
  '',
].join('\n');

const STACKS: readonly Stack[] = [
  {
    name: 'vitest',
    stack: /^vitest@4$/u,
    base: () => Promise.resolve({
      'package.json': JSON.stringify({ name: 'fixture', version: '0.0.0', devDependencies: { vitest: '4.1.11' } }),
      'src/sum.ts': 'export const sum = (a: number, b: number): number => a + b;\n',
      'src/sum.test.ts': VITEST_TEST,
    }),
    head: {
      'package.json': JSON.stringify({ name: 'fixture', version: '0.0.1', devDependencies: { vitest: '4.1.11' } }),
      'src/sum.ts': 'export const sum = (a: number, b: number): number => a + b;\nexport const one = (): number => 1;\nexport const two = (): number => 2;\n',
      'src/sum.test.ts': [
        "import { expect, test } from 'vitest';",
        "import { sum } from './sum';",
        "test('adds', () => { expect(sum(1, 2)).toBeDefined(); });",
        "test.skip('adds zero', () => { expect(sum(0, 0)).toBe(0); });",
        '',
      ].join('\n'),
    },
    suites: ['src/sum.test.ts'],
    testFile: 'src/sum.test.ts',
    deletedCase: 'adds one',
    configFile: 'package.json',
    report: {
      file: 'coverage-final.json',
      text: JSON.stringify({
        '/workspace/src/sum.ts': {
          path: '/workspace/src/sum.ts',
          statementMap: { 0: { start: { line: 1, column: 20 }, end: { line: 1, column: 58 } }, 1: { start: { line: 2, column: 20 }, end: { line: 2, column: 39 } }, 2: { start: { line: 3, column: 20 }, end: { line: 3, column: 39 } } },
          s: { 0: 1, 1: 1, 2: 0 },
        },
      }),
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'pytest',
    stack: /^pytest@8$/u,
    base: async () => ({ ...(await pythonFixture()), 'hello/__init__.py': 'def add(a, b):\n    return a + b\n', 'tests/test_hello.py': PYTEST_TEST }),
    head: {
      'hello/__init__.py': 'def add(a, b):\n    return a + b\n\n\ndef one():\n    return 1\n',
      'tests/test_hello.py': [
        'import pytest',
        'from hello import add',
        '', '',
        'def test_adds():',
        '    assert add(1, 2) != 4',
        '', '',
        '@pytest.mark.skip',
        'def test_adds_zero():',
        '    assert add(0, 0) == 0',
        '',
      ].join('\n'),
    },
    suites: ['tests/test_hello.py'],
    testFile: 'tests/test_hello.py',
    deletedCase: 'test_adds_one',
    configFile: 'pyproject.toml',
    report: {
      file: 'coverage.json',
      text: JSON.stringify({ meta: { format: 3 }, files: { 'hello/__init__.py': { executed_lines: [1, 2, 5], missing_lines: [6], excluded_lines: [] } } }),
    },
  },
];

async function trees(stack: Stack): Promise<{ base: string; head: string; done: () => Promise<void> }> {
  const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), `conformance-r29-${stack.name}-`));
  const base = join(root, 'base');
  const head = join(root, 'head');
  for (const [path, text] of Object.entries(await stack.base())) {
    await mkdir(dirname(join(base, path)), { recursive: true });
    await writeFile(join(base, path), text);
  }
  await cp(base, head, { recursive: true });
  // The Python row's config change appends to the fixture's own pyproject.toml.
  const pyproject = stack.name === 'pytest' ? { 'pyproject.toml': `${await readFile(join(base, 'pyproject.toml'), 'utf8')}\n# changed\n` } : {};
  const changes = { ...stack.head, ...pyproject };
  for (const [path, text] of Object.entries(changes)) await writeFile(join(head, path), text);
  return { base, head, done: () => rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }) };
}

async function eachStack(body: (stack: Stack, base: string, head: string) => Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
  for (const stack of STACKS) {
    const { base, head, done } = await trees(stack);
    try {
      await body(stack, base, head);
    } finally {
      await done();
    }
  }
}

export const EVERY_STACK_HAS_ITS_CONTROLS: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I5.every-supported-stack-has-its-controls',
  title:
    'over a vitest repository and a pytest repository alike, the adapter set names its stack, carries a test, coverage, manifest, and static-analysis adapter, enumerates exactly the suites its runner collects, selects a named test, and names a runner the tree has not installed rather than starting it (R29)',
  run: async () => {
    const { buildAdapterSet } = await adapters();
    await eachStack(async (stack, base) => {
      const set = await buildAdapterSet(base, { provider: null, coverage: { report: join(base, stack.report.file), sourceRoot: '/workspace' } });
      const missing = [set.test === null ? 'test' : null, set.coverage === null ? 'coverage' : null, set.manifest === null ? 'manifest' : null, set.staticAnalysis === null ? 'static-analysis' : null].filter((m) => m !== null);
      if (set.test === null || missing.length > 0) throw new Error(`I5: the ${stack.name} set lacks ${missing.join(', ')}: ${set.reasons.get('test') ?? ''}`);
      if (!stack.stack.test(set.stack)) throw new Error(`I5: the ${stack.name} set named its stack ${set.stack}`);
      const suites = (await set.test.enumerateSuites(base)).map((s) => s.slice(base.length + 1).replaceAll('\\', '/'));
      if (JSON.stringify(suites) !== JSON.stringify(stack.suites)) throw new Error(`I5: the ${stack.name} set enumerated ${JSON.stringify(suites)}`);
      const selected = await set.test.selectSuites(base, [stack.testFile]);
      if (JSON.stringify(selected) !== JSON.stringify([stack.testFile])) throw new Error(`I5: the ${stack.name} set selected ${JSON.stringify(selected)} for ${stack.testFile}`);
      if ((await set.test.missingRunner(base)) === null) throw new Error(`I5: the ${stack.name} set found a runner in a tree nothing installed`);
    });
  },
});

export const EVERY_STACK_REPORTS_TAMPERING: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I3.every-supported-stack-reports-tampering',
  title:
    "over a vitest repository and a pytest repository alike, the runtime's tamper analysis reports a weakened assertion, an added skip, a deleted test, and a changed config file, each by the stack's own adapter, and the assertion is spelled in the stack's language (R29)",
  run: async () => {
    const { analyzeTamper } = await api();
    await eachStack(async (stack, base, head) => {
      const report = await analyzeTamper(base, head, { protectedPaths: [], coverage: null, commands: [], markers: [] });
      const weakened = report.assertionsWeakened.filter((w) => w.file === stack.testFile && w.after !== '(removed)');
      if (weakened.length === 0) throw new Error(`I3: the ${stack.name} set reported no weakened assertion: ${JSON.stringify(report.assertionsWeakened)}`);
      const spelled = stack.name === 'pytest' ? /assert add\(1, 2\) != 4/u : /expect\(sum\(1, 2\)\)\.toBeDefined/u;
      if (!weakened.some((w) => spelled.test(w.after))) throw new Error(`I3: the ${stack.name} weakening was spelled ${JSON.stringify(weakened)}`);
      if (!report.skipMarkersAdded.some((m) => m.file === stack.testFile)) throw new Error(`I3: the ${stack.name} set reported no added skip: ${JSON.stringify(report.skipMarkersAdded)}`);
      if (!report.testsDeleted.some((t) => t.includes(stack.deletedCase))) throw new Error(`I3: the ${stack.name} set reported no deleted test: ${JSON.stringify(report.testsDeleted)}`);
      if (!report.protectedPathsTouched.includes(stack.configFile)) throw new Error(`I3: the ${stack.name} config change was not a touch: ${JSON.stringify(report.protectedPathsTouched)}`);
    });
  },
});

export const EVERY_STACK_MEASURES_CHANGED_LINES: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I3.every-supported-stack-measures-changed-lines',
  title:
    "over a vitest repository and a pytest repository alike, changed-line coverage is read from the stack's own report and counts a changed statement the report shows unrun as uncovered (R29)",
  run: async () => {
    const { buildAdapterSet } = await adapters();
    await eachStack(async (stack, base, head) => {
      const report = join(head, stack.report.file);
      await writeFile(report, stack.report.text);
      const set = await buildAdapterSet(base, { provider: null, coverage: { report, sourceRoot: '/workspace' } });
      if (set.coverage === null) throw new Error(`I3: the ${stack.name} set has no coverage adapter`);
      const measured = await set.coverage.changedLineCoverage(base, head);
      if (!(measured > 0 && measured < 1)) throw new Error(`I3: the ${stack.name} set measured ${String(measured)} over a change with one covered and one uncovered line`);
    });
  },
});

export const DEPENDENCY_TREES_ARE_NOT_CARRIED: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I3.no-stack-dependency-tree-is-carried',
  title:
    "a task's write under node_modules, .venv, site-packages, or __pycache__, at any depth and in any case, is a dependency-tree write, recorded and never carried into the tree a check runs over, whatever the run's stack; the Python installs remove the same trees before installing (R29, D-R15-05)",
  run: async () => {
    const { inDependencyTree } = await api();
    const { UV_INSTALLER, PIP_INSTALLER } = await adapters();
    for (const path of ['node_modules/vitest/vitest.mjs', '.venv/bin/python', 'pkg/__pycache__/m.cpython-313.pyc', 'lib/site-packages/x.py', '.VENV/bin/python']) {
      if (!inDependencyTree(path)) throw new Error(`I3: ${path} was not read as a dependency-tree write, so it would be carried`);
    }
    for (const path of ['src/venv.py', 'hello/__init__.py', 'tests/test_cache.py']) {
      if (inDependencyTree(path)) throw new Error(`I3: ${path} was read as a dependency-tree write`);
    }
    for (const installer of [UV_INSTALLER, PIP_INSTALLER]) {
      for (const tree of ['.venv', '__pycache__', 'site-packages']) {
        if (!installer.trees.includes(tree)) throw new Error(`I3: the ${installer.stack} install does not remove ${tree} before it installs`);
      }
    }
  },
});

export const PYTHON_INSTALL_RUNS_NO_PACKAGE_CODE: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I3.python-install-runs-no-package-code',
  title:
    "each Python install makes its virtualenv with copies, so no link leaves the tree, then installs only wheels verified by the lockfile's hashes, building no package and installing no project; a lockfile naming a package from git, one with no wheel, an unpinned or unhashed requirement, or an option line is refused, naming it (R29, D-R29-05)",
  run: async () => {
    const { UV_INSTALLER, PIP_INSTALLER, UNSUPPORTED_LOCKFILES } = await adapters();
    for (const installer of [UV_INSTALLER, PIP_INSTALLER]) {
      if (installer.steps[0].join(' ') !== 'python -m venv --copies .venv') throw new Error(`I3: the ${installer.stack} install's first step is ${installer.steps[0].join(' ')}`);
    }
    const uv = UV_INSTALLER.steps[1]?.join(' ') ?? '';
    for (const flag of ['--frozen', '--no-build', '--no-install-workspace']) if (!uv.includes(flag)) throw new Error(`I3: the uv install runs without ${flag}: ${uv}`);
    const pip = PIP_INSTALLER.steps[1]?.join(' ') ?? '';
    for (const flag of ['--require-hashes', '--no-deps', '--only-binary :all:']) if (!pip.includes(flag)) throw new Error(`I3: the pip install runs without ${flag}: ${pip}`);
    const hash = `sha256:${'a'.repeat(64)}`;
    const lock = [
      'version = 1', '',
      '[[package]]', 'name = "fromgit"', 'version = "1.0"', 'source = { git = "https://example.com/x.git" }', '',
      '[[package]]', 'name = "sdistonly"', 'version = "1.0"', 'source = { registry = "https://pypi.org/simple" }', 'sdist = { url = "https://x/s.tar.gz", hash = "sha256:00" }', '',
    ].join('\n');
    const read = UV_INSTALLER.packages(lock);
    if (!read.ok || read.packages.some((p) => p.unverifiable === null)) throw new Error(`I3: uv.lock's git and source-only packages were read as verifiable: ${JSON.stringify(read)}`);
    const reqs = PIP_INSTALLER.packages(`requests>=2 --hash=${hash}\nidna==3.0\n`);
    if (!reqs.ok || reqs.packages.some((p) => p.unverifiable === null)) throw new Error(`I3: an unpinned or unhashed requirement was read as verifiable: ${JSON.stringify(reqs)}`);
    const option = PIP_INSTALLER.packages(`--extra-index-url https://example.com/simple\nidna==3.0 --hash=${hash}\n`);
    if (option.ok) throw new Error('I3: a requirements file naming another index was read');
    for (const name of ['poetry.lock', 'Pipfile.lock', 'pdm.lock']) if (!UNSUPPORTED_LOCKFILES.includes(name)) throw new Error(`I3: ${name} is not refused`);
  },
});

/** The rig's stub builder, writing `files` through the sandbox it was handed with the image's own Python, and claiming them. */
async function writingPython(files: Readonly<Record<string, string>>, sandbox: SandboxProvider): Promise<Driver> {
  const driver = await stubDriver();
  return new Proxy(driver, {
    get(target, key, receiver) {
      if (key !== 'runTask') return Reflect.get(target, key, receiver) as unknown;
      return async (req: TaskRequest): Promise<TaskResult> => {
        if (req.role.role !== ('builder' as RoleId)) return target.runTask(req);
        const script = `import json\nfor path, text in json.loads(${JSON.stringify(JSON.stringify(files))}).items():\n    open(path, "w").write(text)\n`;
        const wrote = await sandbox.exec(req.sandbox, ['python', '-c', script]);
        if (wrote.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(`the builder's write did not land: ${wrote.stderr}`);
        const result = await target.runTask(req);
        return { ...result, claim: { ...result.claim, filesChanged: Object.keys(files) } };
      };
    },
  });
}

export const PYTHON_RUN_REACHES_INTEGRATE: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I3.python-run-reaches-integrate',
  title:
    "a run on a Python repository reaches integrate on the Docker provider: the runtime installs its uv.lock from PyPI in the Python image, the locked pytest acceptance test fails over the base and passes over the builder's change in that image with no egress, and the admission records the stack as pytest@8 (R29, needs Docker and the network)",
  timeoutMs: 1_200_000,
  run: async () => {
    const [{ startRun, readRunRecords }, { INSTALLERS, PYPI_REGISTRIES, PYTHON_IMAGE, STATIC_ANALYSIS_IMAGE }, { LocalDockerProvider }, { BASE_IMAGE }] = await Promise.all([
      api(), adapters(), import('@olympus-ai/sandbox'), import('@olympus-ai/driver-claude-code'),
    ]);
    await withLine('r29-line-', async (rig) => {
      const root = rig.dirs.artifacts;
      for (const [path, text] of Object.entries(await pythonFixture())) {
        await mkdir(dirname(join(root, path)), { recursive: true });
        await writeFile(join(root, path), text);
      }
      const test = 'tests/test_hello.py';
      // Semgrep reads Python: the manifest pins a Python rule, as it pins TypeScript ones for a Node.js repository (R17).
      const rules = 'rules:\n  - id: no-eval\n    languages: [python]\n    severity: ERROR\n    message: eval of a value\n    pattern: eval($X)\n';
      await writeManifest(rig.dirs, [
        { id: 'greets', kind: 'acceptance', onBase: 'fails', tests: [test], required: true, timeoutMs: 300_000 },
        { id: 'static-analysis', kind: 'static-analysis', image: STATIC_ANALYSIS_IMAGE, config: rules, required: true, timeoutMs: 300_000 },
      ]);
      const sandbox = await LocalDockerProvider.create({ vaultPaths: [rig.dirs.store, rig.dirs.artifacts] });
      const driver = await writingPython({ 'hello/__init__.py': 'def greet(name):\n    return "hello " + name\n' }, sandbox);
      const components = await rig.components({
        sandbox, driver, reviewer: driver, installers: INSTALLERS,
        // The profile's check image is Node's, with no Python in it: a check that ran there, not in the Python set's own image, could not pass.
        profile: { buildImage: PYTHON_IMAGE, checkImage: BASE_IMAGE, limits: { cpus: 2, memoryMb: 2048, pids: 512 } },
      }, { work: false });
      const policy = await linePolicy({}, { installRegistries: [...PYPI_REGISTRIES] });
      const request = await rig.request(components, { policy, artifacts: { ...ARTIFACTS, acceptanceTests: [...ARTIFACTS.acceptanceTests, test] } });
      const parked = refusalOf(await startRun(request), 'R29 python line');
      if (parked.at !== 'integrate' || parked.transition.reason !== 'approval-required') {
        const baseRun = await components.vault.readBaseRun(rig.runId, null);
        const record = baseRun === null ? null : await readRecord<BaseRunRecord>(components.vault, baseRun);
        const seen = record === null ? 'no base record' : JSON.stringify({ checks: record.checks.map((c) => ({ id: c.checkId, exit: c.exitCode, out: `${c.stdout}${c.stderr}`.slice(-600) })), unstarted: record.unstarted });
        throw new Error(`R29: the Python run stopped at ${parked.at} as '${parked.transition.reason}', not at integrate awaiting approval; base: ${seen}`);
      }
      const records = await readRunRecords(components.vault, rig.runId);
      if (records.stack !== 'pytest@8') throw new Error(`R29: the run recorded its stack as ${String(records.stack)}`);
    });
  },
});

 /**
  * Every field is required: a scan that defaulted its image, its limits, or

 export interface ScanOptions {
   /** A git repository on the host. Read, never written. */

   /** The revision scanned, resolved to a commit before anything runs. */

 /** The ceiling a report derived. A report always carries one; only `NOT_SCANNED` stands for none. */

 type Adapters = { readonly set: AdapterSet; readonly reason: string | null } | { readonly set: null; readonly reason: string };
 
+/**
+ * Whether the tree is probed as a Python repository: its set is pytest's, or
+ * it has no package.json and holds a pyproject.toml or a requirements file
+ * (D-R29-09). Every other tree is probed as a Node.js one, as before R29.
+ */
+function isPython(adapters: Adapters, files: readonly string[]): boolean {
+  if (adapters.set?.test instanceof PytestAdapter) return true;
+  if (adapters.set?.test !== null && adapters.set?.test !== undefined) return false;
+  if (files.includes('package.json')) return false;
+  return files.some((f) => f === 'pyproject.toml' || /^requirements[\w.-]*\.txt$/u.test(f));
+}
+
+/** Python's lockfiles, the supported two and the ones refused, by name at the root. */
+const PYTHON_LOCKFILES = ['uv.lock', 'requirements.txt', 'poetry.lock', 'Pipfile.lock', 'pdm.lock'];
+
+/**
+ * One supported Python lockfile at the root, whose every package the
+ * installer can verify by hash, as the line's own install requires (R34,
+ * D-R29-05).
+ */
+async function pythonPinned(tree: string, files: readonly string[]): Promise<{ readonly observation: Observation; readonly installer: DependencyInstaller | null }> {
+  const evidence: ProbeEvidence = { via: 'static', read: 'the tree listing and the Python lockfile' };
+  const locks = PYTHON_LOCKFILES.filter((name) => files.includes(name));
+  if (locks.length === 0) return { observation: { outcome: 'absent', detail: 'no uv.lock or hashed requirements.txt is committed, so a clean install cannot reproduce the dependencies', evidence }, installer: null };
+  if (locks.length > 1) return { observation: { outcome: 'absent', detail: `several lockfiles are committed (${locks.join(', ')}), and which is authoritative is not something to guess`, evidence }, installer: null };
+  const installer = [UV_INSTALLER, PIP_INSTALLER].find((i) => i.lockfile === locks[0]);
+  if (installer === undefined) return { observation: { outcome: 'absent', detail: `${locks.join('')} is a lockfile no installer reads`, evidence }, installer: null };
+  const read = installer.packages(await readFile(join(tree, installer.lockfile), 'utf8'));
+  if (!read.ok) return { observation: { outcome: 'absent', detail: read.reason, evidence }, installer: null };
+  const unverifiable = read.packages.filter((p) => p.unverifiable !== null);
+  if (unverifiable.length > 0) {
+    return { observation: { outcome: 'absent', detail: `${installer.lockfile} does not pin every package by a hash an install can verify: ${unverifiable.slice(0, 3).map((p) => `${p.key} (${p.unverifiable ?? ''})`).join('; ')}`, evidence }, installer: null };
+  }
+  return { observation: { outcome: 'supported', detail: `dependencies are pinned by ${installer.lockfile}`, evidence }, installer };
+}

+/** The declared installer's steps, each in turn over the scan's copy, in the installer's image, reaching PyPI alone (D-R29-09). */
+async function pythonInstall(ctx: Context, installer: DependencyInstaller | null): Promise<Observation> {
+  if (installer === null) {
+    return { outcome: 'absent', detail: 'there is no single pinned Python lockfile to install from', evidence: { via: 'not-run', because: 'build.pinned-manifest' } };
+  }
+  let last: Observation | undefined;
+  for (const step of installer.steps) {
+    // The installer's cache is under /tmp, which a fresh container holds empty; its HOME is the scan's.
+    const env = { ...installer.env, HOME: `${MOUNT}/home` };
+    last = judged(await execute(ctx, inTree(step), {
+      image: installer.image, env, egress: { mode: 'allowlist', allow: [...ctx.options.pythonRegistries] }, wallClockMs: ctx.options.timeouts.installMs,
+    }), `${installer.stack} install from ${installer.lockfile} (${step.slice(0, 3).join(' ')})`);
+    if (last.outcome !== 'supported') return last;
+  }
+  return last ?? { outcome: 'absent', detail: 'the installer declares no step', evidence: { via: 'static', read: installer.lockfile } };
+}
+
+/**
+ * pytest at the base, judged by the JUnit report it writes, or coverage.py
+ * over the same run, judged by its JSON report: an exit 0 says nothing failed,
+ * and the report says whether anything ran (I5).
+ */
+async function pythonSuite(ctx: Context, test: AdapterSet['test'], kind: 'green' | 'coverage'): Promise<Observation> {
+  if (!(test instanceof PytestAdapter)) return { outcome: 'indeterminate', detail: 'no pytest adapter, so no suite can run', evidence: { via: 'not-run', because: 'testing.adapter' } };
+  const name = `${kind}-${randomUUID()}`;
+  await mkdir(join(ctx.scanDir, 'out', name));
+  const out = `${MOUNT}/out/${name}`;
+  const pytest = test.invocation(null).slice(3);
+  const python = { image: PYTHON_IMAGE, env: { PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE: '1' } };
+  if (kind === 'green') {
+    const what = 'the suite at base';
+    const ran = judged(await execute(ctx, inTree(['.venv/bin/python', '-m', 'pytest', ...pytest, `--junitxml=${out}/results.xml`]), python), what);
+    if (ran.outcome !== 'supported') return ran;
+    const counts = await junitCounts(join(ctx.scanDir, 'out', name, 'results.xml'));
+    if (counts === null) return { ...ran, outcome: 'absent', detail: `${what} exited 0 and wrote no readable report` };
+    if (counts.failed > 0) return { ...ran, outcome: 'absent', detail: `${what} exited 0 and its report counts ${String(counts.failed)} failed` };
+    if (counts.passed < 1) return { ...ran, outcome: 'absent', detail: `${what} exited 0 and no test passed: every test was skipped or deselected` };
+    return { ...ran, detail: `${what} exited 0 with ${String(counts.passed)} test(s) passed` };
+  }
+  const what = 'the suite under coverage.py, writing a JSON report';
+  const data = `--data-file=${out}/.coverage`;
+  const ran = judged(await execute(ctx, inTree(['.venv/bin/python', '-m', 'coverage', 'run', data, '-m', 'pytest', ...pytest]), python), what);
+  if (ran.outcome !== 'supported') return ran;
+  const wrote = judged(await execute(ctx, inTree(['.venv/bin/python', '-m', 'coverage', 'json', data, '-o', `${out}/coverage.json`]), python), 'the coverage.py JSON report');
+  if (wrote.outcome !== 'supported') return wrote;
+  const report = await readReport(join(ctx.scanDir, 'out', name, 'coverage.json'));
+  const files = report?.files;
+  const measured = typeof files === 'object' && files !== null ? Object.keys(files).length : 0;
+  if (measured === 0) return { ...ran, outcome: 'absent', detail: `${what} exited 0 and measured no file` };
+  return { ...ran, detail: `${what} exited 0 and measured ${String(measured)} file(s)` };
+}
+
+/** The counts on a JUnit report's `testsuite` elements, summed, or null for anything that is not one. */
+async function junitCounts(path: string): Promise<{ readonly passed: number; readonly failed: number } | null> {
+  let text: string;
+  try {
+    if (!(await lstat(path)).isFile()) return null;
+    text = await readFile(path, 'utf8');
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+  const suites = [...text.matchAll(/<testsuite\b([^>]*)>/gu)];
+  if (suites.length === 0) return null;
+  let tests = 0;
+  let failed = 0;
+  let skipped = 0;
+  for (const [, attributes = ''] of suites) {
+    const count = (name: string): number | null => {
+      const m = new RegExp(`\\b${name}="(\\d+)"`, 'u').exec(attributes);
+      return m?.[1] === undefined ? null : Number(m[1]);
+    };
+    const t = count('tests');
+    if (t === null) return null;
+    tests += t;
+    failed += (count('failures') ?? 0) + (count('errors') ?? 0);
+    skipped += count('skipped') ?? 0;
+  }
+  return { passed: tests - failed - skipped, failed };
+}
+

   const scripts = manifest.kind === 'read' ? manifest.scripts : {};
   if (typeof scripts.build === 'string') return judged(await execute(ctx, inTree([...runner, 'run', 'build'])), 'the build script in a fresh container');

===== packages/readiness/test/scan.test.ts =====
diff --git a/packages/readiness/test/scan.test.ts b/packages/readiness/test/scan.test.ts
index e1f5bd2..69d8afc 100644
--- a/packages/readiness/test/scan.test.ts
+++ b/packages/readiness/test/scan.test.ts
@@ -185,3 +185,42 @@ describe('a revision', () => {
     });
   });
 });
+
+/** The Python fixture: pytest and coverage.py pinned by a uv.lock with every wheel hashed, one module, one passing test (R29). */
+async function pythonRepository(dir: string, extra: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {}): Promise<void> {
+  const { readFile } = await import('node:fs/promises');
+  const fixture = join(import.meta.dirname, 'fixtures', 'python');
+  const files: Record<string, string> = {};
+  for (const path of ['pyproject.toml', 'uv.lock', 'hello/__init__.py', 'tests/test_hello.py']) files[path] = await readFile(join(fixture, ...path.split('/')), 'utf8');
+  await gitRepository(dir, { ...files, 'AGENTS.md': '# Agents\n', ...extra });
+}
+
+describe('a Python repository (R29)', () => {
+  it('clears L2: pinned by uv.lock, installed by uv in the Python image, compiled, green and measured at base, with tamper analysis', async () => {
+    await withWorkbench(async (bench) => {
+      const dir = join(bench.base, 'python');
+      await pythonRepository(dir);
+      const report = await scan(options(bench, dir, { branchProtection: PROTECTED, protectedPaths: ['pyproject.toml', 'uv.lock'] }));
+      for (const id of ['build.pinned-manifest', 'build.install', 'build.clean', 'testing.adapter', 'testing.enumerate', 'testing.green-at-base', 'testing.coverage', 'testing.tamper-analysis'] as const) {
+        expect(probe(report, id).outcome, `${id}: ${probe(report, id).detail}`).toBe('supported');
+      }
+      expect(probe(report, 'testing.adapter').detail).toMatch(/pytest@8/);
+      const install = probe(report, 'build.install').evidence;
+      expect(install.via === 'executed' ? install.image : '').toMatch(/^ghcr\.io\/astral-sh\/uv@sha256:/u);
+      expect(report.ceiling, renderReport(report)).toMatchObject({ kind: 'scanned', level: 2 });
+    });
+  });
+
+  it('holds a Python repository at L0 on its failing suite, and one with no lockfile at its manifest', async () => {
+    await withWorkbench(async (bench) => {
+      const failing = join(bench.base, 'failing');
+      await pythonRepository(failing, { 'tests/test_hello.py': 'def test_fails():\n    assert 1 == 2\n' });
+      const red = await scan(options(bench, failing, { branchProtection: PROTECTED }));
+      expect(red.ceiling).toMatchObject({ level: 0, heldBy: { kind: 'probe', probe: 'testing.green-at-base' } });
+      const unpinned = join(bench.base, 'unpinned');
+      await gitRepository(unpinned, { 'pyproject.toml': '[project]\nname = "x"\nversion = "0"\n[dependency-groups]\ndev = ["pytest"]\n' });
+      const loose = await scan(options(bench, unpinned, { branchProtection: PROTECTED }));
+      expect(loose.ceiling).toMatchObject({ level: 0, heldBy: { kind: 'probe', probe: 'build.pinned-manifest' } });
+    });
+  });
+});

 /** A-R34-02: the runtime's install, whole: the lockfile's hashes, its diff by package, and the installed trees' digest. */
 function isInstallRecord(i: unknown): boolean {
   return isRecord(i) && i.collectedBy === 'runtime' && hasString(i, 'stack') && hasString(i, 'lockfile') && hasString(i, 'image') &&
-    isStrings(i.invocation) && (i.invocation as unknown[]).length > 0 &&
+    // A-R29-02: every step the install ran, in order; none empty, and at least one.
+    Array.isArray(i.steps) && i.steps.length > 0 && i.steps.every((s) => isStrings(s) && (s as unknown[]).length > 0) &&
     typeof i.lockfileSha256 === 'string' && SHA256_HEX.test(i.lockfileSha256) &&
     (i.baseLockfileSha256 === null || (typeof i.baseLockfileSha256 === 'string' && SHA256_HEX.test(i.baseLockfileSha256))) &&
     isRecord(i.lockfileDiff) && isStrings(i.lockfileDiff.added) && isStrings(i.lockfileDiff.removed) && isStrings(i.lockfileDiff.changed) &&
     typeof i.installedTreeSha256 === 'string' && SHA256_HEX.test(i.installedTreeSha256);
 }

===== packages/vault/test/local.test.ts =====
diff --git a/packages/vault/test/local.test.ts b/packages/vault/test/local.test.ts
index 66699bf..c050f79 100644
--- a/packages/vault/test/local.test.ts
+++ b/packages/vault/test/local.test.ts
@@ -372,78 +372,82 @@ describe('enforcement decisions (A-P14-01)', () => {
 describe('the base record (D-A-BF-01)', () => {
   const reproduced = { checkId: 'reproduces', exitCode: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '', suiteCount: 1, expectation: null, findings: null, durationMs: 1, startedAt: '2026-10-11T00:00:00.000Z' };
   const record = {
     runId,
     baseTreeSha256: 'c'.repeat(64),
     checks: [reproduced],
     unstarted: [],
     collectedBy: 'runtime' as const,
     draft: null,
     install: null,
   };
 
   test('is absent until written, then read back by the reference it was written under', async () => {
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, null)).toBeNull();
     const ref = await vault.recordBaseRun(record);
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, null)).toEqual(ref);
     expect(JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(await vault.read(ref)))).toEqual(record);
   });
 
   test('is written once: a second base for the run is refused and the first stands', async () => {
     const ref = await vault.recordBaseRun(record);
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, checks: [{ ...reproduced, exitCode: 0 }] })).rejects.toThrow(/already has a base record/);
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, null)).toEqual(ref);
   });
 
   test('refuses a record the runtime did not collect, and stores nothing', async () => {
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, collectedBy: 'agent' } as never)).rejects.toThrow(/collectedBy/);
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, null)).toBeNull();
   });
 
   test('R34: stores a whole install, and refuses one that is not the runtime\'s or names no tree digest', async () => {
     const install = {
-      stack: 'npm', lockfile: 'package-lock.json', image: `node@sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`, invocation: ['npm', 'ci', '--ignore-scripts'],
+      stack: 'npm', lockfile: 'package-lock.json', image: `node@sha256:${'d'.repeat(64)}`, steps: [['npm', 'ci', '--ignore-scripts']],
       lockfileSha256: 'e'.repeat(64), baseLockfileSha256: null, lockfileDiff: { added: ['node_modules/a'], removed: [], changed: [] },
       installedTreeSha256: 'f'.repeat(64), collectedBy: 'runtime' as const,
     };
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, install: { ...install, collectedBy: 'agent' } } as never)).rejects.toThrow(/install/);
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, install: { ...install, installedTreeSha256: 'x' } })).rejects.toThrow(/install/);
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, install: undefined } as never)).rejects.toThrow(/install/);
+    // A-R29-02: the steps it ran, at least one and none empty.
+    await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, install: { ...install, steps: [] } })).rejects.toThrow(/install/);
+    await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, install: { ...install, steps: [['python', '-m', 'venv', '--copies', '.venv'], []] } })).rejects.toThrow(/install/);
+    await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, install: { ...install, invocation: ['npm', 'ci'], steps: undefined } } as never)).rejects.toThrow(/install/);
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, null)).toBeNull();
     const ref = await vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, install });
     expect(ref.kind).toBe('base-run');
   });
 
   test('refuses a result whose suite count is absent, since the suite-count rule would read it as met (codex-obs), and stores nothing', async () => {
     const { suiteCount: _omitted, ...uncounted } = reproduced;
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, checks: [uncounted] } as never)).rejects.toThrow(/not results/);
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, checks: [{ ...reproduced, suiteCount: -1 }] })).rejects.toThrow(/not results/);
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, checks: [{ ...reproduced, suiteCount: 1.5 }] })).rejects.toThrow(/not results/);
     // R17: a security check's findings are the base set, so one the runtime could not have written is refused.
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, checks: [{ ...reproduced, findings: [{ rule: 'r', location: { path: 'a', line: 1 }, fingerprint: 'not-a-digest' }] }] })).rejects.toThrow(/not results/);
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, null)).toBeNull();
   });
 
   test('refuses an exit code that is not an integer: NaN is written as null and read back as a failure', async () => {
     for (const exitCode of [Number.NaN, Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY, 1.5]) {
       await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, checks: [{ ...reproduced, exitCode }] })).rejects.toThrow(/not results/);
     }
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, null)).toBeNull();
   });
 
   test('is written once per draft hash beside the admitted checks\' (R19), and a draft\'s record is not the run\'s', async () => {
     const draft = 'a'.repeat(64);
     const admitted = await vault.recordBaseRun(record);
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, draft)).toBeNull();
     const drafted = await vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, draft });
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, draft)).toEqual(drafted);
     expect(await vault.readBaseRun(runId, null)).toEqual(admitted);
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, draft, checks: [{ ...reproduced, exitCode: 0 }] })).rejects.toThrow(/already has a base record for draft/);
     expect(JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(await vault.read(drafted)))).toEqual({ ...record, draft });
     await expect(vault.recordBaseRun({ ...record, draft: 'not-a-hash' })).rejects.toThrow(/draft hash/);
   });
 
   test('a reference whose hash is not the record\'s reads nothing', async () => {
     await vault.recordBaseRun(record);
     await expect(vault.read({ runId, kind: 'base-run', hash: 'd'.repeat(64) })).rejects.toThrow(/hashes to/);
   });
 });
